tests, validation used formatting, Clippy -D warnings, release builds and native profiling.

slabs showed no repeatable improvement; default stays 64.

They do not establish exact pixel parity or broader device coverage.

===== Intermediate looping reserves: incorrect cast-ID selection (not final policy) =====
This stage added separate looping keys but incorrectly selected some status art by the
casting ability ID. The Inner Fire census still showed 53 cold roots: the visual lookup
uses the applied modifier ID instead. Final selection follows the versioned AbilityEffect
modifier and duration, and is recorded separately below.

===== Intermediate modifier-correct reserves with path-priority allocation =====
Inner Fire cold roots disappear, but its long-lived occupancy consumes the reserve budget
before missile requests: baseline A still has 50 cold Mortar missiles and 10 Gryphon missiles.
This is not the final allocation policy. Final allocation shares the finite budget across
activation waves before granting extra concurrent occupancy, retaining prior reservations
during population growth.
